`attorney`: manages assigned clients and matters, writes privileged notes, and reviews documents.\n191: - `paralegal`: manages tasks, uploads documents, and drafts client communications for assigned matters.\n192: - Privileged notes and client-visible messages.\n201: - Deadline/calendar records and reminders.\n202: \n203: ## Feature Capabilities\n204: \n205: - Matter intake and conflict-check-like workflow.\n206: - Client portal for shared documents, messages, and matter status.\n210: - Internal privileged notes separated from client-visible messages.\n211: - Search and filters across matters, clients, tasks, documents, and deadlines.\n212: - CSV import for clients and matters.\n213: - CSV export for matter status, deadlines, and document inventory.\n214: - Audit log for document access, note creation, exports, sharing changes, and role changes.\n215: \n216: ## Security-Relevant Data\n217: \n218: - Client PII and matter details.\n219: - Privileged attorney notes.\n220: - Confidential documents and versions.\n221: - Deadlines and legal strategy metadata.\n222: - Client communications.\n223: \n224: ## Expected Real-World Complexity\n225: \n226: -
